```
1	---
2	created: 2026-06-16T00:00:00.000Z
3	title: SDK client self-bootstrap folder tree from root IPNS key
4	area: architecture
5	severity: medium
6	files:
7	  - packages/sdk/src/types.ts:44
8	  - packages/sdk/src/client.ts:288
9	  - packages/sdk/src/client.ts:345
10	  - packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts:323
11	  - apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts:73
12	  - apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts:128
13	  - apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts:96
14	---
15	
16	## Problem
17	
18	`CipherBoxClient` is positioned as the stateful owner of all folder operations
19	(holds `folderTree`, `binState`, `keyCache`, `vaultKeypair`, `rootFolderKey`),
20	but `CipherBoxClientConfig` (`packages/sdk/src/types.ts:44`) carries
21	`rootIpnsName` + `rootFolderKey` + `vaultKeypair` and **NOT** the root IPNS
22	private key. The root IPNS keypair lives only in the web `vaultStore`. So the
23	client cannot resolve/publish root or lazy-load folders from root on its own —
24	the web app must seed `folderTree` via `ensureFolderRegistered`
25	(`apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts:96`) before every folderTree-dependent client
26	method (`replaceFile`, `restoreFileVersion`, `deleteFileVersion`,
27	`restoreFromBin`, `renameItem`, `deleteItem`, `uploadFile(s)`, `moveItem`,
28	`createFolder`, `shareFolder`).
29	
30	This asymmetry is the root cause of the whole "Folder not loaded" bug class.
31	Every folderTree-dependent method throws `'Folder not loaded'` (or
32	`'Target folder not loaded'`) when the web forgot to seed it. The PR #494
33	regression was simply three of those seeding calls being dropped (fixed on branch
34	`fix/owner-edit-folder-not-loaded` by adding `ensureFolderRegistered` to
35	`useFileOperations` + `useFileVersions`).
36	
37	### Concrete still-open instance: bin restore
38	
39	Restoring a recycle-bin item after a client re-creation (page reload / re-login)
40	throws `'Target folder not loaded'` when the item's original parent folder was
41	never navigated to that session. Throw site: `packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts:323`
42	(`restoreFromBin` derefs `folderTree.get(targetFolderIpnsName)` to get the parent
43	key + ipnsKeypair needed to republish the parent). Web callers
44	`apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts:73` (`restore`) and `:128` (`restoreMultiple`)
45	never register the target folder. **Pre-existing since PR #296**, not a #494
46	regression. Not caught by `tests/web-e2e/tests/recycle-bin.spec.ts` because that
47	test navigates/uploads first (parent already in `folderTree`) and never reloads.
48	
49	A localized guard isn't enough here: bin entries carry only
50	`originalParentIpnsName`, and after a reload the parent may not be in the Zustand
51	store at all, so there is nothing to register. The parent's keys live in *its*
52	parent's `FolderEntry`, so loading it by ipnsName requires walking from root.
53	
54	## Solution
55	
56	Make the stateful client able to bootstrap and lazy-load on its own, dissolving
57	the `ensureFolderRegistered` workaround class:
58	
59	1. Add `rootIpnsKeypair` (root IPNS private key) to `CipherBoxClientConfig`. The
60	   web passes it from `vaultStore` at `initSdkClient`. Security: no new exposure —
61	   the client already holds `vaultKeypair.privateKey`, `rootFolderKey`, and every
62	   loaded folder's `ipnsPrivateKey` in memory (zeroed on `destroy()`); the root
63	   ipns private key is consistent with that.
64	2. Have the client self-register root (resolve + register) on init or lazily.
65	3. Implement an internal `ensureFolderLoaded(targetIpnsName)` that DFS-walks the
66	   folder tree from root, unwrapping each `FolderEntry`'s keys with the vault
67	   keypair and loading metadata (`loadFolder`/`loadFolderMetadata`) until the
68	   target is registered.
69	4. Call `ensureFolderLoaded` inside the folderTree-dependent methods — definitely
70	   `restoreFromBin`, and as a safety net for `replaceFile` / `restoreFileVersion`
71	   / `deleteFileVersion` / `renameItem` / `deleteItem` / `uploadFile(s)` /
72	   `moveItem`. Then the web-side `ensureFolderRegistered` calls can be removed.
73	
74	### Tests
75	
76	- SDK unit test for `ensureFolderLoaded`: deep target, target-not-found,
77	  already-loaded (no-op).
78	- New web E2E: reload → open bin → restore an item whose subfolder parent was
79	  never navigated to (the scenario `recycle-bin.spec.ts` misses).
80	
81	### Related
82	
83	- `[[route-shared-folder-writes-through-the-sdk-client]]` — adjacent SDK-client
84	  ownership cleanup (shared-write paths). Both move state authority into the SDK.
85	- The #494 hotfix (`fix/owner-edit-folder-not-loaded`) is the symptom patch this
86	  todo would supersede.
87	
```

## SDK client self-bootstrap folder tree from root IPNS key

**Area:** architecture · **Severity:** medium · **Created:** today
**Files:** 7 referenced — `packages/sdk/src/types.ts:44`, `client.ts:288/345`, `bin/index.ts:323`, `apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts:73/128`, `lib/sdk-provider.ts:96`

### Problem (summary)
`CipherBoxClient` owns folder state (`folderTree`) but its config carries `rootIpnsName` + `rootFolderKey` + `vaultKeypair` and **not** the root IPNS private key — that lives only in the web `vaultStore`. So the client can't resolve/publish root or lazy-load folders on its own; the web must seed `folderTree` via `ensureFolderRegistered` before every folderTree-dependent method. That asymmetry is the root cause of the whole **"Folder not loaded"** bug class. Still-open instance: **bin restore** after reload (`restoreFromBin` at `bin/index.ts:323`) throws `'Target folder not loaded'` — pre-existing since PR #296, not caught by `recycle-bin.spec.ts`.

### Solution (summary)
1. Add `rootIpnsKeypair` to `CipherBoxClientConfig` (web passes from `vaultStore`; no new key exposure).
2. Client self-registers root on init/lazily.
3. Internal `ensureFolderLoaded(targetIpnsName)` DFS-walks from root, unwrapping each `FolderEntry`'s keys.
4. Call it inside folderTree-dependent methods → remove web-side `ensureFolderRegistered`.

### Roadmap match
This maps to **Phase 47: SDK folder-state and publish-path consolidation** (REQ-1, "make the SDK client the single source of truth") — same "move state authority into the SDK" theme. Phase 47 is planned (5 plans, `47-01`..`47-05`) but its plan checkboxes are still unchecked. It also links to pending todo **#8** (`route-shared-folder-writes-through-the-sdk-client`) — adjacent SDK-ownership cleanup. This todo is a *superset extension* of Phase 47 REQ-1 (adds self-bootstrap so `ensureFolderRegistered` can be deleted), not literally one of its 5 plans.

Files: `packages/sdk/src/types.ts`, `packages/sdk/src/client.ts`, `packages/sdk/src/state/folder-tree.ts`.\n\n## 1. `CipherBoxClientConfig` (types.ts:44-88)\n\n| Field | Type | Optional | Notes |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `apiUrl` | `string` | required | API base URL |\n| `getAccessToken` | `() => Promise<string>` | required | consumer owns refresh |\n| `vaultKeypair` | `{ publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array }` | required | secp256k1, ECIES |\n| `rootIpnsName` | `string` | required | root folder IPNS **name** only |\n| `rootFolderKey` | `Uint8Array` | required | AES-256 root folder key |\n| `teeKeys` | `TeeKeys` | optional | IPNS key wrapping |\n| `shareCallbacks` | `ShareCallbacks` | optional | re-wrapping hooks |\n| `onOperationStart` | `(operation: string) => void` | optional | |\n| `onOperationEnd` | `(operation: string) => void` | optional | |\n| `onError` | `(error: Error) => void` | optional | |\n| `defaultHeaders` | `Record<string, string>` | optional | |\n| `axiosInstance` | `AxiosInstance` | optional | shared orval singleton |\n| `pinningConfig` | `PinningConfig` | optional | BYO-IPFS |\n\n**Critical finding for self-bootstrap:** there is **NO root IPNS private key / keypair** in the config today. Only `rootIpnsName` (string identifier, types.ts:51-52) and `rootFolderKey` (types.ts:53-54). The config carries the *name* but not the Ed25519 *signing key* for the root folder. This is exactly the gap noted in MEMORY (\"SDK config lacks the root IPNS key so the client can't self-bootstrap\"). The vault `privateKey` is secp256k1 (ECIES/decryption), not an IPNS signing key.\n\n```ts\n// types.ts:51-54\n/** Root folder IPNS name (k51.../bafzaa...) */\nrootIpnsName: string;\n/** Root folder key (AES-256, decrypted on login) */\nrootFolderKey: Uint8Array;\n```\n\n## 2. CipherBoxClient private fields (client.ts:52-63)\n\n```ts\nprivate config: CipherBoxClientConfig;                                           // 52\nprivate ctx: SdkContext;                                                         // 53\nprivate emitter: SdkEventEmitter;                                               // 54\nprivate folderTree: FolderTree;                                                 // 55  -- wraps Map<string, FolderState>\nprivate keyCache: KeyCache;                                                     // 56\nprivate binState: BinState | null = null;                                       // 57\nprivate externalProvider: PinningProvider | null = null;                        // 59\nprivate internalVaultKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array };// 61\nprivate internalRootFolderKey: Uint8Array;                                      // 62\n```\n\nNotes vs. the names asked for:\n- **folderTree**: type is the `FolderTree` class (client.ts:55), which internally is `Map<string, FolderState>` keyed by `ipnsName` (folder-tree.ts:21). Value shape = `FolderState` (see §3).\n- **vaultKeypair / rootFolderKey**: there are no fields by those literal names. The constructor takes them from `config` and stores **defensive copies** as `internalVaultKeypair` (client.ts:61) and `internalRootFolderKey` (client.ts:62). The originals also live in `this.config` (re-pointed to the internal copies — client.ts:71-75).\n- **rootIpnsName**: NOT stored as a dedicated field. It lives only in `this.config.rootIpnsName`. There is no field tracking the root folder's IPNS keypair anywhere.\n- **keyCache**: `KeyCache` instance (client.ts:56), cleared on destroy but not otherwise touched by folder ops in this file.\n\n## 3. folderTree entry value shape — `FolderState` (types.ts:96-111)\n\nThe folderTree value type is **`FolderState`**, defined in `types.ts:96-111` (NOT `FolderEntry`; `FolderEntry` from `@cipherbox/core` is the metadata child record, a different type used in children arrays — imported at client.ts:27).\n\n```ts\nexport type FolderState = {\n  ipnsName: string;                                              // 98\n  folderKey: Uint8Array;                                         // 100  decrypted AES-256\n  ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array };// 102  Ed25519 IPNS signing key\n  sequenceNumber: bigint;                                        // 104  IPNS version clock\n  children: FolderChild[];                                       // 106  files + subfolders\n  metadata: FolderMetadata | null;                              // 108  null until full load\n  lastLoadedAt: number;                                          // 110  ms\n};\n```\n\nKey points:\n- The per-folder **IPNS signing keypair IS held here** (`ipnsKeypair`, types.ts:102) — so non-root folders get their signing key into folderTree at registration/creation time. Root folder has no such entry until something registers it.\n- **No parent pointer.** Tree linkage is implicit via `children` (each child of type `'folder'` is a `FolderEntry` carrying its own `ipnsName`); traversal is done by looking up child `ipnsName` in folderTree (see `collectSubtreeIpnsNames`, client.ts:209-222).\n- `children` holds `FolderChild[]` = union of `FilePointer` (`type:'file'`, has `fileMetaIpnsName`, `ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted`) and `FolderEntry` (`type:'folder'`, has `ipnsName`). Cast sites at client.ts:185-188, 214-218.\n\n## 4. Methods that WRITE folderTree\n\nDirect `folderTree.set(...)` / `registerFolder` / `loadFolder` writers:\n\n| Method | client.ts | Signature / how it writes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `registerFolder` | 288-308 | `registerFolder(ipnsName: string, folderKey: Uint8Array, ipnsKeypair: {publicKey;privateKey}, children: FolderChild[], sequenceNumber: bigint): void` — bridge method; builds a FolderState with `metadata: null`, `folderTree.set(ipnsName, {...})` (296). The external-load equivalent of `ensureFolderRegistered`. |\n| `loadFolder` | 345-381 | `async loadFolder(ipnsName, folderKey, ipnsKeypair): Promise<FolderState \\| null>` — loads from IPNS via `sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata`, sets full FolderState incl. `metadata`, `folderTree.set` (369). |\n| `createFolder` | 394-485 | mutates parent (435-438 `folderTree.set(parentIpnsName, parent)`); the new subfolder is published to IPNS but **NOT** inserted into folderTree (only returned to caller). |\n| `renameItem` | 496-537 | re-sets parent folder state (526). |\n| `moveItem` | 549-611 | re-sets source + dest (592-593). |\n| `deleteItem` | 623-668 | re-sets folder (652). |\n| `uploadFile` | 685-849 | re-sets folder (805). |\n| `uploadFiles` | 866-1117 | re-sets folder (1067). |\n| `replaceFile` | 1145-1226 | re-sets folder (1213). |\n| `restoreFileVersion` | 1255-1315 | re-sets folder (1304). |\n| `deleteFileVersion` | 1338-1402 | re-sets folder (1391). |\n\nThere is **no auto/lazy load** path — every writer except `loadFolder` requires the folder to already be present. `registerFolder`/`loadFolder` are the only two true entry points that create a folderTree entry from outside; both require the caller to supply the `ipnsKeypair`. There is **no method that bootstraps the root folder from config** (no read of `config.rootIpnsName` into folderTree anywhere in this file).\n\nUnderlying `FolderTree.set` (folder-tree.ts:29-38) deep-clones `folderKey` and `ipnsKeypair` to protect caller buffers.\n\n## 5. Methods that READ folderTree and throw on missing folder\n\nEvery reader uses `this.folderTree.get(...)` then throws. Exact strings:\n\n| Method | Line | Throw string |\n|---|---|---|\n| `createFolder` | client.ts:400 | `'Parent folder not loaded'` |\n| `renameItem` | client.ts:499 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `moveItem` | client.ts:553 | `'Source folder not loaded'` |\n| `moveItem` | client.ts:554 | `'Destination folder not loaded'` |\n| `deleteItem` | client.ts:626 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `uploadFile` | client.ts:694 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `uploadFiles` | client.ts:888 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `replaceFile` | client.ts:1162 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `restoreFileVersion` | client.ts:1274 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `deleteFileVersion` | client.ts:1357 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n| `shareFolder` | client.ts:1723 | `'Folder not loaded'` |\n\n(The todo's cited lines 288/345 in the prompt correspond to the current `registerFolder`/`loadFolder` definitions — line numbers have shifted; the actual throwers are the table above.) Non-throwing readers that tolerate absence: `getFolderSequenceNumber` (255), `getFolderIpnsPrivateKey` (267), `collectSubtreeIpnsNames` (211, returns early if absent), `hasFolder` (247), and bin/share event emitters that use `?.children ?? []` (e.g. 1568-1574, 1604-1610). Bin/share ops receive `this.folderTree` by reference (`deleteToBin` 1560, `restoreFromBin` 1597) and resolve folders internally.\n\n## 6. Constructor / init path (client.ts:64-103)\n\n- **No async init.** Construction is fully synchronous; there is no `init()`/bootstrap method. State (folderTree) starts empty.\n- Key material handling (64-75): makes defensive copies — `internalVaultKeypair` (66-69) and `internalRootFolderKey` (70), then rebuilds `this.config` spreading the original config but **overriding** `vaultKeypair` and `rootFolderKey` to point at the internal copies (71-75). So all downstream reads of `this.config.vaultKeypair`/`this.config.rootFolderKey` hit the zeroable internal copies.\n- `rootIpnsName` is **never extracted or used** in the constructor — it just rides along inside `this.config` via the spread (72). It is not consumed anywhere in client.ts.\n- Builds `axiosInstance` (76-82, reuses `config.axiosInstance` if provided), `ctx` (83-87), `emitter` (88), `folderTree` (89), `keyCache` (90).\n- Sets up `externalProvider` from `pinningConfig` (93-102): kubo→`KuboProvider`, pinata→`PinataProvider`, else→`PsaProvider`.\n- `rootFolderKey` is later read by `getBinContext()` (client.ts:1866) — that's its only consumer in this file. `vaultKeypair` is read in createFolder/uploadFile/uploadFiles (userPublicKey), download paths (userPrivateKey, 1473/1509), and bin/share contexts (1864-1865, 1875-1876).\n\n## 7. destroy() / zeroization (client.ts:228-238)\n\n```ts\ndestroy(): void {\n  this.folderTree.clear();               // 229\n  this.keyCache.clear();                 // 230\n  this.emitter.removeAll();              // 231\n  this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);  // 234\n  this.internalVaultKeypair.publicKey.fill(0);   // 235\n  this.internalRootFolderKey.fill(0);            // 236\n  this.binState = null;                  // 237\n}\n```\n\n- `folderTree.clear()` (folder-tree.ts:60-66) iterates every FolderState and fills `folderKey` and `ipnsKeypair.privateKey` with zeros, then clears the Map. (`FolderTree.delete` does the same per-entry, 41-49.)\n- Per-field zeroing only touches the **internal copies** (`internalVaultKeypair`, `internalRootFolderKey`), never the caller's buffers — by design, because the constructor copied them (60-62 comment).\n- **Consistency requirement for a new `rootIpnsKeypair`:** to match this pattern, any added root IPNS keypair field must (a) be defensively copied in the constructor like `internalVaultKeypair`, (b) be `.fill(0)`-zeroed in `destroy()`, and (c) if it lands inside a folderTree entry's `ipnsKeypair`, it is already covered by `folderTree.clear()`. A root keypair stored only as a standalone client field would need an explicit `.privateKey.fill(0)` (and `.publicKey.fill(0)`) added to `destroy()`. Note `FolderTree.set` clones keypairs (folder-tree.ts:33-36), so a root entry inserted via set gets an independent buffer that `clear()` will zero.\n\n### Summary of the bootstrap gap\nThe root folder is special: config provides `rootIpnsName` + `rootFolderKey` but **no root IPNS signing keypair**, and nothing in the constructor seeds folderTree. Every mutation/read method requires a prior `registerFolder`/`loadFolder` call supplying an `ipnsKeypair`. For the root specifically there is no signing key available to the client, so it cannot self-load the root from IPNS or sign root updates without the web tier passing the root `ipnsKeypair` in explicitly."

Here is the dense map.\n\n---\n\n## CipherBox: Folder Load + Key-Unwrap Map\n\nKey finding: the SDK packages (`packages/sdk`, `packages/sdk-core`) operate on **already-unwrapped** keys. The actual ECIES unwrap of a child `FolderEntry`'s keys lives in the **web app** (`apps/web`), not the SDK. There is **no** root-to-target DFS/walk helper anywhere.\n\n### 1. Function(s) that resolve+load a folder by ipnsName\n\n**`loadFolderMetadata`** — `packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts:73-94`\n```ts\nasync function loadFolderMetadata(params: { ipnsName: string; folderKey: Uint8Array; ctx: SdkContext })\n  : Promise<{ metadata: FolderMetadata; sequenceNumber: bigint; cid: string } | null>\n```\n- Calls `resolveIpnsRecord(ipnsName, ctx)` → `{cid, sequenceNumber}`; returns `null` if not found.\n- Calls `fetchAndDecryptMetadata(cid, folderKey, ctx)`.\n- Returns decrypted `{metadata, sequenceNumber, cid}`. **Takes the already-decrypted `folderKey` as input — does no unwrapping.**\n\n**`fetchAndDecryptMetadata`** — `packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts:49-63`: `fetchFromIpfs(ctx, cid)` → parse v1 JSON `{iv, data}` → `decryptFolderMetadata(encrypted, folderKey)` (AES-256-GCM). Note: all folder metadata (incl. root) is v1 JSON; v2 blob format is only the vault key blob.\n\n**`resolveIpnsRecord`** — `packages/sdk-core/src/ipns/index.ts:182-239`: IPNS resolve via `ipnsControllerResolveRecord`, verifies Ed25519 signature + that pubKey derives to the requested name, returns `{cid, sequenceNumber, signatureVerified}` or `null` on 404.\n\n**Client wrapper `CipherBoxClient.loadFolder`** — `packages/sdk/src/client.ts:345-381`:\n```ts\nasync loadFolder(ipnsName: string, folderKey: Uint8Array,\n  ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array }): Promise<FolderState | null>\n```\nDelegates to `sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata`, stores `FolderState` in `folderTree`, emits `folder:loaded`. **Receives both `folderKey` and `ipnsKeypair` pre-unwrapped from the caller.**\n\n### 2. Folder metadata schema + how child folder keys are stored\n\nCite: **`packages/core/src/folder/types.ts`**.\n\n- `FolderMetadata` (`:15-20`): `{ version: 'v2'; children: FolderChild[] }` — entire object AES-256-GCM encrypted as one blob.\n- `FolderChild = FolderEntry | FilePointer` (`:25`).\n- **`FolderEntry`** (subfolder) (`:31-47`) — carries the wrapped child keys inside the parent's metadata:\n  ```ts\n  type FolderEntry = {\n    type: 'folder'; id: string; name: string;\n    ipnsName: string;                    // child subfolder IPNS name (k51...)\n    ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: string;     // hex ECIES-wrapped Ed25519 IPNS private key\n    folderKeyEncrypted: string;          // hex ECIES-wrapped AES-256 folder key\n    createdAt: number; modifiedAt: number;\n  }\n  ```\nBoth wrapped fields are produced in `createSubfolder` (`packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts:104-167`) via `wrapKey(..., userPublicKey)` then `bytesToHex(...)` (lines 123-126, 144-153). (`FilePointer` carries only `ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted` + `fileMetaIpnsName`, no folderKey.)\n\n### 3. Exact crypto call to UNWRAP a child folder's keys with the vault keypair\n\nThe SDK never does this — the caller passes plaintext keys in. The canonical unwrap is in the web app:\n\n**`apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderNavigation.ts:233-240`**\n```ts\nconst folderKey = await unwrapKey(\n  hexToBytes(folderEntry.folderKeyEncrypted),\n  vaultKeypair.privateKey\n);\nconst ipnsPrivateKey = await unwrapKey(\n  hexToBytes(folderEntry.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted),\n  vaultKeypair.privateKey\n);\n```\n`vaultKeypair` comes from `useAuthStore.getState().vaultKeypair` (`:205`).\n\n**`unwrapKey(wrappedKey: Uint8Array, privateKey: Uint8Array): Promise<Uint8Array>`** — `packages/crypto/src/ecies/decrypt.ts:23-54`. ECIES over secp256k1 via `eciesjs` `decrypt(privateKey, wrappedKey)` (ECDH + HKDF + AES-GCM). Generic `CryptoError` on any failure (oracle-attack hardening). Same primitive used for the rootFolderKey in `packages/sdk-core/src/vault/index.ts:78`.\n\n### 4. How an ipnsKeypair is reconstructed from a decrypted ipnsPrivateKey\n\nThere is **no eager reconstruction of the public key.** The decrypted 32-byte Ed25519 private key seed is stored directly, and the public key is derived lazily only when publishing:\n\n- Web registers the folder via **`ensureFolderRegistered`** (`apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts:96-122`), passing the keypair as:\n  ```ts\n  { publicKey: new Uint8Array(0),   // \"Public key derived from private key when needed\" (:116)\n    privateKey: folder.ipnsPrivateKey }\n  ```\n- The client stores this verbatim in `FolderTree` (`packages/sdk/src/client.ts:288-308` `registerFolder` / `:359-369` `loadFolder` → `FolderTree.set`, `packages/sdk/src/state/folder-tree.ts:29-38`).\n- Public key is derived on demand at publish time via **`deriveEd25519PublicKey(privateKey)`**:\n  - `packages/sdk-core/src/ipns/index.ts:62` (`createAndPublishIpnsRecord`)\n  - `packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts:411` (`buildFolderIpnsRecord`, the `params.ipnsPublicKey ?? deriveEd25519PublicKey(...)` fallback)\n  - `apps/web/src/services/ipns.service.ts:57`\n\nSo the \"keypair object the client stores\" is `{ publicKey: Uint8Array (often length-0 placeholder), privateKey: <decrypted ipnsPrivateKey> }`; `publicKey` is reconstructed via `deriveEd25519PublicKey` at signing time, not at load time.\n\n(For comparison, the root/vault keypair IS built with a real public key elsewhere — e.g. `deriveVaultKeyIpnsKeypair` in `packages/sdk-core/src/vault/index.ts:32` — but child FolderEntry keypairs use the length-0 placeholder pattern above.)\n\n### 5. DFS / recursive \"walk from root to a target ipnsName\" helper\n\n**No such helper exists.** Confirmed across `packages/sdk/src`, `packages/sdk-core/src`, and `apps/web/src`. What exists is unrelated:\n\n- `packages/sdk-core/src/folder/tree.ts` exports `getDepth`, `calculateSubtreeDepth`, `isDescendantOf` (re-exported at `packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts:37`) — depth/ancestry checks on a `TreeNode`, **not** an IPNS resolve-and-descend walk.\n- `apps/web/src/services/search-index.service.ts:312-322` `buildPath` walks the **parent chain upward** over already-loaded `FolderNode`s in the Zustand store (`node.parentId`) to build a breadcrumb string — in-memory, upward, no IPNS resolution, no key unwrapping.\n- `packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts:383` explicitly notes recursive folder cleanup is **not** handled because it \"requires deep IPNS traversal.\"\n\nFolder descent is currently driven entirely by the web UI navigating one level at a time (`useFolderNavigation.ts`): resolve child IPNS → unwrap that child's keys → load. No code resolves a chain from root down to an arbitrary target `ipnsName` in one call.\n\n### Relevant file paths\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/folder/index.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/ipns/index.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/vault/index.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk-core/src/folder/tree.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk/src/client.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk/src/state/folder-tree.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/core/src/folder/types.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/crypto/src/ecies/decrypt.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderNavigation.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts`\n- `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/apps/web/src/services/search-index.service.ts`"

16	      "text": "# Recycle-Bin Restore Map — `packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts`\n\n## 1. `restoreFromBin` — signature, body, folderTree deref, throw\n\n**Signature** (`packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts:301-307`):\n```ts\nexport async function restoreFromBin(params: {\n  entryId: string;\n  targetFolderIpnsName: string;\n  folderTree: FolderTree;\n  binState: BinState;\n  binCtx: BinOperationContext;\n}): Promise<{ restoredItem: FolderChild; updatedBinState: BinState }>\n```\n\n**Flow:**\n- **Find bin entry** (`:309-310`): `binState.entries.find(e => e.id === entryId)`; throws `'Bin entry not found'` if absent.\n- **Reconstruct FolderChild** (`:313-320`): for `itemType === 'file'` uses `entry.filePointer`, for `'folder'` uses `entry.folderEntry` (each spread with fresh `modifiedAt: Date.now()`); else throws `'Bin entry has no stored item reference'`.\n- **folderTree deref** (`:323-324`) — the line the todo describes:\n  ```ts\n  const targetFolder = params.folderTree.get(params.targetFolderIpnsName);\n  if (!targetFolder) throw new Error('Target folder not loaded');\n  ```\n  This is keyed on `targetFolderIpnsName` (the restore destination, NOT necessarily `entry.originalParentIpnsName`). `FolderTree.get` returns `FolderState | undefined` (`packages/sdk/src/state/folder-tree.ts:24`).\n- **What it derefs from the resolved entry** — the `FolderState` (`packages/sdk/src/types.ts:96-111`) supplies the parent key + ipnsKeypair used for republish:\n  - `targetFolder.folderKey` → AES folder key (`:346`)\n  - `targetFolder.ipnsKeypair.privateKey` → Ed25519 IPNS signing key (`:347`)\n  - `targetFolder.sequenceNumber`, `targetFolder.children` (`:327, :338, :348`)\n- **Name-collision handling** (`:327-336`): if `child.name` already in `targetFolder.children`, renames to `\"${name} (restored)\"`, then `(restored 2)`, `(restored 3)`…\n- **Republish parent** (`:342-356`): calls `sdkCore.updateFolderMetadataAndPublish` with `children = [...targetFolder.children, child]`, `baseChildren`, `folderKey`, `ipnsPrivateKey`, `ipnsName`, `sequenceNumber`; then writes the merged result back into folderTree (`folder.children = publishedChildren`, `sequenceNumber`, `lastLoadedAt`, `folderTree.set(...)` — the CR-01 \"adopt merged published set\" pattern).\n- **Republish bin** (`:359-374`): filters entry out of `binState.entries`, `sequenceNumber + 1`, `saveBinMetadata(...)`, returns `{ restoredItem: child, updatedBinState }`.\n\n## 2. Bin entry data model — keys vs ipnsName\n\nType: `BinEntry` in `packages/core/src/bin/types.ts:19-49`.\n\nRelevant fields:\n```ts\n/** IPNS name of the original parent folder (for restore path resolution) */\noriginalParentIpnsName: string;   // :26-27\noriginalPath: string;             // :28-29  breadcrumb only\nfilePointer?: FilePointer;        // :45-46  preserved FolderChild (file)\nfolderEntry?: FolderEntry;        // :47-48  preserved FolderChild (folder)\n```\n\n**Carries ipnsName ONLY — no parent KEYS.** `originalParentIpnsName` is a bare string identifier. There is **no** `folderKey`, no `ipnsKeypair`/`ipnsPrivateKey`, no parent-key material anywhere on `BinEntry`. The entry only stores the child payload (`filePointer`/`folderEntry`) plus CID/size bookkeeping (`contentCid`, `contentSize`, `versionCids`). The parent folder's `folderKey` + `ipnsKeypair` needed to republish must come from `folderTree.get(...)` → `FolderState` at restore time — hence the `'Target folder not loaded'` throw when folderTree wasn't seeded. This is the same \"Folder not loaded\" failure class noted in memory (the SDK can't self-bootstrap the parent's keys from the bin entry).\n\n`RecycleBinMetadata` (`packages/core/src/bin/types.ts:58-65`): `{ version: 'v1'; sequenceNumber: number; entries: BinEntry[] }`.\n\n## 3. `restoreMultiple`\n\n**Not present.** No `restoreMultiple`, `restoreAll`, or batch-restore function exists in `bin/index.ts`. Exported functions are only: `loadBin`, `addToBin`, `restoreFromBin`, `permanentDeleteFromBin`, `emptyBin`, `purgeExpiredEntries`. Restore is strictly single-entry per call.\n\n## 4. Other folderTree-reading methods that can throw the same way\n\nOnly one other site reads folderTree, and it throws an analogous error:\n\n- **`addToBin`** (`packages/sdk/src/bin/index.ts:224-293`):\n  ```ts\n  const folder = params.folderTree.get(params.folderIpnsName);   // :232\n  if (!folder) throw new Error('Folder not loaded');             // :233\n  ```\n  Same dependency: needs `folder.folderKey` + `folder.ipnsKeypair.privateKey` (`:246-247`) from `FolderState` to republish the parent after removing the child. Here is the map.\n\n---\n\n# Web Folder-Seeding + Vault Store Map\n\n## 1. `ensureFolderRegistered` — full implementation\n\n**File:** `apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts:96-122`\n\nBridges Zustand folder state → SDK `folderTree`. Reads from the `FolderNode` argument (not directly from a store; caller pulls the node from `useFolderStore`). Logic:\n\n1. `getSdkClient()` (throws if not initialized).\n2. If `client.hasFolder(folder.ipnsName)` → **return** (SDK folderTree is authoritative, no reconcile).\n3. Guard: if `folder.folderKey.length === 0 || folder.ipnsPrivateKey.length === 0` → **return** (skip placeholder folders with empty key material).\n4. Else `client.registerFolder(...)`.\n\n```ts\nexport function ensureFolderRegistered(folder: FolderNode): void {\n  const client = getSdkClient();\n  if (client.hasFolder(folder.ipnsName)) {\n    return;\n  }\n  if (folder.folderKey.length === 0 || folder.ipnsPrivateKey.length === 0) {\n    return;\n  }\n  client.registerFolder(\n    folder.ipnsName,\n    folder.folderKey,\n    {\n      publicKey: new Uint8Array(0), // Public key derived from private key when needed\n      privateKey: folder.ipnsPrivateKey,\n    },\n    folder.children,\n    folder.sequenceNumber\n  );\n}\n```\n\n`registerFolder` signature (`packages/sdk/src/client.ts:288-308`): `(ipnsName, folderKey, ipnsKeypair{publicKey,privateKey}, children, sequenceNumber)` — sets `folderTree[ipnsName]` with defensive copies. Note web passes an **empty publicKey** (`new Uint8Array(0)`); SDK derives it from the private key when needed.\n\n### Every web call site of `ensureFolderRegistered`\n\n| File:line | Operation context |\n|---|---|\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts:8` | import |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useDropUpload.ts:109` | before drop-upload into `parentFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts:8` | import |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts:66` | version op (`parentFolder`) |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileVersions.ts:181` | version op (`parentFolder`) |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:6` | import |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:114` | `parentFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:171` | `parentFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:217-218` | `sourceFolder` + `destFolder` (move/copy) |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:301-302` | `sourceFolder` + `destFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:346` | `parentFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:396` | `parentFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFolderMutations.ts:425` | `freshParent` (conditional) |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts:7` | import |\n| `apps/web/src/hooks/useFileOperations.ts:80` | `parentFolder` |\n| `apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts:96` | definition |\n\n**Not called from `useBin.ts`** (the bug — see §4).\n\n---\n\n## 2. `initSdkClient` — config passed today\n\n**Definition:** `apps/web/src/lib/sdk-provider.ts:26-35` (injects shared `apiAxios` as `axiosInstance`, stores `_lastConfig`).\n\n**Sole call site:** `apps/web/src/hooks/useAuth.ts:299-324`. Config literal passed into `CipherBoxClientConfig`:\n\n```ts\nconst sdkClient = initSdkClient({\n  apiUrl,                                   // VITE_API_URL || window.location.origin + '/api'  (useAuth.ts:282)\n  getAccessToken,                           // closure over useAuthStore.getState().accessToken   (useAuth.ts:283-286)\n  vaultKeypair: {                           // from userKeypair (Web3Auth-derived secp256k1)\n    publicKey: userKeypair.publicKey,\n    privateKey: userKeypair.privateKey,\n  },\n  rootIpnsName: vaultState.rootIpnsName,    // useVaultStore  (vault.store.ts)\n  rootFolderKey: vaultState.rootFolderKey,  // useVaultStore  (vault.store.ts)\n  teeKeys: authState.teeKeys ?? undefined,  // useAuthStore\n  pinningConfig,                            // loadByoConfig(...)  (useAuth.ts:289-290)\n  shareCallbacks: { getCoveringShares, addShareKeys },  // share.service closures (useAuth.ts:310-323)\n});\n```\n\nGuarded by `if (vaultState.rootFolderKey && vaultState.rootIpnsKeypair && vaultState.rootIpnsName)` at `useAuth.ts:281`.\n\n**Config field source map vs `CipherBoxClientConfig` (`packages/sdk/src/types.ts:44-88`):**\n\n| Config field | Source | Notes |\n|---|---|---|\n| `apiUrl` | env / origin | `useAuth.ts:282` |\n| `getAccessToken` | `useAuthStore.getState().accessToken` | `useAuth.ts:283-286` |\n| `vaultKeypair` | `userKeypair` (secp256k1) | NOT the IPNS keypair |\n| `rootIpnsName` | `useVaultStore` → `vaultState.rootIpnsName` | |\n| `rootFolderKey` | `useVaultStore` → `vaultState.rootFolderKey` | |\n| `teeKeys` | `useAuthStore` → `authState.teeKeys` | |\n| `pinningConfig` | `loadByoConfig` | |\n| `shareCallbacks` | `share.service` | |\n| `axiosInstance` | injected in `initSdkClient` | `sdk-provider.ts:33` |\n\n**Key gap:** the config does **not** include the root IPNS keypair. `CipherBoxClientConfig` (types.ts:44-88) has **no** `rootIpnsKeypair` field — only `vaultKeypair` (the user's secp256k1 ECIES keypair), `rootIpnsName`, and `rootFolderKey`. So the SDK client cannot self-bootstrap/lazy-load the root folder into its `folderTree`; it relies on `registerFolder`/`ensureFolderRegistered` from the web side. This is the missing field a new `rootIpnsKeypair` config would fill.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Vault store — holds the root IPNS keypair (private key)\n\n**File:** `apps/web/src/stores/vault.store.ts` — `useVaultStore` (Zustand, memory-only).\n\nField (`vault.store.ts:7-10, 15`):\n\n```ts\ntype IpnsKeypair = {\n  publicKey: Uint8Array;\n  privateKey: Uint8Array;     // Ed25519 IPNS signing private key\n};\ntype VaultState = {\n  rootFolderKey: Uint8Array | null;\n  rootIpnsKeypair: IpnsKeypair | null;   // <-- holds root IPNS private key\n  rootIpnsName: string | null;\n  ...\n};\n```\n\n**Populated via** `setVaultKeys` (`vault.store.ts:57-65`) — called by the login flow after decrypting `rootIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted` with the user's private key (`vault.store.ts:38-43` docstring). **Zero-filled** on logout in `clearVaultKeys` (`vault.store.ts:68-93`): `rootIpnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0)` and `.publicKey.fill(0)`.\n\n**Confirmed:** `useVaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair` is a full Ed25519 keypair (incl. `privateKey`). This is exactly what a new `rootIpnsKeypair` config field would receive — and unlike the web's `ensureFolderRegistered` call (which passes `publicKey: new Uint8Array(0)`), the store has the real `publicKey` too. Currently `initSdkClient` (useAuth.ts:299) does **not** pass `vaultState.rootIpnsKeypair` into the SDK config, even though it's available and the guard at `useAuth.ts:281` already checks it is non-null.\n\n---\n\n## 4. `useBin.ts` — restore paths do NOT call `ensureFolderRegistered` (open bug confirmed)\n\n**File:** `apps/web/src/hooks/useBin.ts`. No import of `ensureFolderRegistered` (line 4 imports only `getSdkClient, hasSdkClient`).\n\n**`restore` (single)** — `useBin.ts:64-81`:\n\n```ts\nconst entry = binEntries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);\nif (!entry) throw new Error('Bin entry not found');\nconst client = getSdkClient();\nawait client.restoreFromBin(entryId, entry.originalParentIpnsName);   // line 73 — NO ensureFolderRegistered\n```\n\n**`restoreMultiple`** — `useBin.ts:120-136`:\n\n```ts\nfor (const entryId of entryIds) {\n  const entry = binEntries.find((e) => e.id === entryId);\n  if (!entry) continue;\n  await client.restoreFromBin(entryId, entry.originalParentIpnsName);  // line 128 — NO ensureFolderRegistered\n}\n```\n\nNeither seeds the target folder (`entry.originalParentIpnsName`) into the SDK `folderTree` before calling `restoreFromBin`.\n\n**Why this breaks:** `restoreFromBin` (`packages/sdk/src/client.ts:1589-1614`) delegates to `binOps.restoreFromBin({ folderTree: this.folderTree, targetFolderIpnsName, ... })`. 1	/**
2	 * @cipherbox/sdk - Internal folder tree state manager
3	 *
4	 * Tracks loaded folders, their decrypted keys, metadata, and IPNS state.
5	 * This is the SDK's internal state -- NOT exposed to consumers directly.
6	 * Consumers receive state changes via events.
7	 *
8	 * Security: clear() fills all key material with zeros before removing
9	 * references, following the memory-clearing principle from CLAUDE.md.
10	 */
11	
12	import type { FolderState } from '../types';
13	
14	/**
15	 * Internal folder tree state. Tracks loaded folders by IPNS name.
16	 *
17	 * Each folder's FolderState includes decrypted keys, children, and
18	 * IPNS sequence number -- everything needed to read and update the folder.
19	 */
20	export class FolderTree {
21	  private folders = new Map<string, FolderState>();
22	
23	  /** Get a folder's state by IPNS name */
24	  get(ipnsName: string): FolderState | undefined {
25	    return this.folders.get(ipnsName);
26	  }
27	
28	  /** Set or update a folder's state, cloning key material to avoid zeroing caller buffers */
29	  set(ipnsName: string, state: FolderState): void {
30	    this.folders.set(ipnsName, {
31	      ...state,
32	      folderKey: new Uint8Array(state.folderKey),
33	      ipnsKeypair: {
34	        publicKey: new Uint8Array(state.ipnsKeypair.publicKey),
35	        privateKey: new Uint8Array(state.ipnsKeypair.privateKey),
36	      },
37	    });
38	  }
39	
40	  /** Remove a folder from the tree */
41	  delete(ipnsName: string): void {
42	    const state = this.folders.get(ipnsName);
43	    if (state) {
44	      // Clear sensitive key material before removing
45	      if (state.folderKey) state.folderKey.fill(0);
46	      if (state.ipnsKeypair?.privateKey) state.ipnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
47	    }
48	    this.folders.delete(ipnsName);
49	  }
50	
51	  /** Check if a folder has been loaded */
52	  has(ipnsName: string): boolean {
53	    return this.folders.has(ipnsName);
54	  }
55	
56	  /**
57	   * Clear all folder state, zeroing all sensitive key material.
58	   * Called during client destroy().
59	   */
60	  clear(): void {
61	    for (const state of this.folders.values()) {
62	      if (state.folderKey) state.folderKey.fill(0);
63	      if (state.ipnsKeypair?.privateKey) state.ipnsKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
64	    }
65	    this.folders.clear();
66	  }
67	
68	  /** Get a snapshot of all loaded folders (for iteration) */
69	  getAll(): Map<string, FolderState> {
70	    return new Map(this.folders);
71	  }
72	}
73

88	    this.emitter = new SdkEventEmitter();
89	    this.folderTree = new FolderTree();
90	    this.keyCache = new KeyCache();

92	    // Initialize BYO pinning provider if configured
93	    if (config.pinningConfig?.mode !== 'cipherbox' && config.pinningConfig?.externalProvider) {
94	      const ext = config.pinningConfig.externalProvider;
95	      if (ext.protocol === 'kubo') {
96	        this.externalProvider = new sdkCore.KuboProvider(ext.endpoint, ext.authToken);
97	      } else if (ext.protocol === 'pinata') {
98	        this.externalProvider = new sdkCore.PinataProvider(ext.endpoint, ext.authToken);
99	      } else {
100	        this.externalProvider = new sdkCore.PsaProvider(ext.endpoint, ext.authToken);
101	      }
102	    }

204	  /**
205	   * Recursively collect all IPNS names from a folder subtree.
206	   * Walks the in-memory folderTree to find file IPNS names and subfolder IPNS names.
207	   * Only collects from loaded folders -- unloaded subtrees are skipped.
208	   */
209	  private collectSubtreeIpnsNames(folderIpnsName: string, acc: string[] = []): string[] {
210	    acc.push(folderIpnsName);
211	    const folder = this.folderTree.get(folderIpnsName);
212	    if (!folder) return acc;
213	
214	    for (const child of folder.children) {
215	      if (child.type === 'file') {
216	        acc.push((child as FilePointer).fileMetaIpnsName);
217	      } else if (child.type === 'folder') {
218	        this.collectSubtreeIpnsNames((child as FolderEntry).ipnsName, acc);
219	      }
220	    }
221	    return acc;
222	  }
223	
224	  /**
225	   * Destroy the client, clearing all sensitive state.
226	   * After calling destroy(), the client instance should not be reused.
227	   */
228	  destroy(): void {
229	    this.folderTree.clear();
230	    this.keyCache.clear();
231	    this.emitter.removeAll();
232	    // Zero internal key copies (defense-in-depth; JS GC may retain copies)
233	    // Only zeroes our copies, not the caller-provided buffers
234	    this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey.fill(0);
235	    this.internalVaultKeypair.publicKey.fill(0);
236	    this.internalRootFolderKey.fill(0);
237	    this.binState = null;
238	  }
239	
240	  /**
241	   * Check if the SDK already has state for a folder.
242	   *
243	   * @param ipnsName - Folder's IPNS name
244	   * @returns true if the folder is registered in the SDK's FolderTree
245	   */
246	  hasFolder(ipnsName: string): boolean {
247	    return this.folderTree.has(ipnsName);
248	  }
249	
250	  /**
251	   * Get the current sequence number for a folder in the SDK's internal state.
252	   * Returns undefined if the folder is not registered.
253	   */
254	  getFolderSequenceNumber(ipnsName: string): bigint | undefined {
255	    return this.folderTree.get(ipnsName)?.sequenceNumber;
256	  }
257	
258	  /**
259	   * Get the IPNS private key for a folder in the SDK's internal state.
260	   * Returns undefined if the folder is not registered or has no key.
261	   *
262	   * Used by ensureFolderRegistered to preserve SDK's correct IPNS key
263	   * when the store has an empty placeholder (SDK-created folders store
264	   * keys internally, not in Zustand).
265	   */
266	  getFolderIpnsPrivateKey(ipnsName: string): Uint8Array | undefined {
267	    const key = this.folderTree.get(ipnsName)?.ipnsKeypair?.privateKey;
268	    // Return a copy so callers can't mutate internal state
269	    return key && key.length > 0 ? new Uint8Array(key) : undefined;
270	  }
271	
272	  /**
273	   * Register an externally-loaded folder into the SDK's internal state.
274	   *
275	   * Used when folder metadata is loaded outside the SDK (e.g., by a navigation
276	   * hook) but the SDK needs to know about the folder for mutation operations
277	   * (create, rename, move, delete, upload).
278	   *
279	   * This is a bridge method for gradual SDK adoption -- eventually all folder
280	   * loading should go through client.loadFolder().
281	   *
282	   * @param ipnsName - Folder's IPNS name
283	   * @param folderKey - Decrypted AES-256 folder key
284	   * @param ipnsKeypair - Ed25519 keypair for IPNS signing
285	   * @param children - Current folder children
286	   * @param sequenceNumber - Current IPNS sequence number
287	   */
288	  registerFolder(
289	    ipnsName: string,
290	    folderKey: Uint8Array,
291	    ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: Uint8Array; privateKey: Uint8Array },
292	    children: FolderChild[],
293	    sequenceNumber: bigint
294	  ): void {
295	    // Defensive copy so destroy() -> folderTree.clear() doesn't zero caller buffers
296	    this.folderTree.set(ipnsName, {
297	      ipnsName,
298	      folderKey: new Uint8Array(folderKey),
299	      ipnsKeypair: {
300	        publicKey: new Uint8Array(ipnsKeypair.publicKey),
301	        privateKey: new Uint8Array(ipnsKeypair.privateKey),
302	      },
303	      sequenceNumber,
304	      children,
305	      metadata: null,
306	      lastLoadedAt: Date.now(),
307	    });
308	  }

334	  /**

340	   * @param ipnsName - Folder's IPNS name

367	      };
368	
369	      this.folderTree.set(ipnsName, state);
370	
371	      this.emitter.emit({

375	        children: result.metadata.children,
376	        sequenceNumber: result.sequenceNumber,
377	      });
378	
379	      return state;
380	    });
381	  }

1888:  private async withOperation<T>(name: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
=== definition ===
1888:   private async withOperation<T>(name: string, fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
1889:     const start = Date.now();
1890:     this.notifySafely(() => this.config.onOperationStart?.(name));
1891:     this.emitter.emit({ type: 'operation:start', operation: name });
1892: 
1893:     try {
1894:       const result = await fn();
1895:       const durationMs = Date.now() - start;
1896:       this.notifySafely(() => this.config.onOperationEnd?.(name));
1897:       this.emitter.emit({ type: 'operation:end', operation: name, durationMs });
1898:       return result;
1899:     } catch (error) {
1900:       this.notifySafely(() => this.config.onError?.(error as Error));
1901:       this.emitter.emit({ type: 'error', operation: name, error: error as Error });
1902:       throw error;
1903:     }
1904:   }
```

id: toolu_012NCDywu14ju9fPZiV7vebV
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk/src/client.ts",
  "old_string": "      return state;\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create a new subfolder inside an existing folder.",
  "new_string": "      return state;\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Ensure a folder is present in the internal folderTree, self-bootstrapping\n   * from root if necessary.\n   *\n   * If the target is already loaded, returns it immediately. Otherwise — when a\n   * root IPNS keypair was configured — walks the folder tree from root (DFS with\n   * early exit), resolving each folder's metadata and unwrapping each subfolder's\n   * `folderKeyEncrypted` / `ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted` with the vault keypair, until\n   * the target is registered. Every folder visited along the way is cached, so\n   * later calls are cheap.\n   *\n   * Returns null when the client cannot self-bootstrap (no `rootIpnsKeypair`\n   * configured) or the target is not reachable from root. Callers fall back to\n   * their existing 'Folder not loaded' error on null, so behavior is unchanged\n   * when self-bootstrap is unavailable. This dissolves the \"Folder not loaded\"\n   * failure class that previously required consumers to pre-seed folderTree\n   * before every folderTree-dependent operation.\n   *\n   * @param targetIpnsName - IPNS name of the folder to ensure is loaded\n   * @returns The loaded FolderState, or null if it cannot be bootstrapped\n   * @internal\n   */\n  async ensureFolderLoaded(targetIpnsName: string): Promise<FolderState | null> {\n    const existing = this.folderTree.get(targetIpnsName);\n    if (existing) return existing;\n\n    // Cannot self-bootstrap without the root IPNS signing key.\n    if (!this.internalRootIpnsKeypair) return null;\n\n    // 1. Ensure root is loaded. Root is special: it has no parent to unwrap its\n    //    keys from, so they come from config (rootFolderKey + rootIpnsKeypair).\n    const rootIpnsName = this.config.rootIpnsName;\n    let root = this.folderTree.get(rootIpnsName);\n    if (!root) {\n      root = await this.loadFolder(\n        rootIpnsName,\n        this.internalRootFolderKey,\n        this.internalRootIpnsKeypair\n      );\n      if (!root) return null;\n    }\n    if (rootIpnsName === targetIpnsName) return root;\n\n    // 2. DFS from root, unwrapping child keys and loading metadata until the\n    //    target is found. `visited` guards against repeats and pathological\n    //    cycles in folder metadata.\n    const visited = new Set<string>([rootIpnsName]);\n    const stack: FolderState[] = [root];\n    while (stack.length > 0) {\n      const current = stack.pop() as FolderState;\n      for (const child of current.children) {\n        if (child.type !== 'folder') continue;\n        const entry = child as FolderEntry;\n        if (visited.has(entry.ipnsName)) continue;\n        visited.add(entry.ipnsName);\n\n        let childState = this.folderTree.get(entry.ipnsName);\n        if (!childState) {\n          // Unwrap this subfolder's keys with the vault keypair (ECIES), then\n          // load its metadata. loadFolder adopts independent clones into the\n          // folderTree (zeroed on destroy()); the transient unwrapped buffers\n          // are left to GC, matching the existing loadFolder/registerFolder\n          // paths which likewise don't eagerly zero their unwrapped inputs.\n          const folderKey = await unwrapKey(\n            hexToBytes(entry.folderKeyEncrypted),\n            this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey\n          );\n          const ipnsPrivateKey = await unwrapKey(\n            hexToBytes(entry.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted),\n            this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey\n          );\n          childState = await this.loadFolder(entry.ipnsName, folderKey, {\n            // Public key is derived from the private key at signing time.\n            publicKey: new Uint8Array(0),\n            privateKey: ipnsPrivateKey,\n          });\n        }\n        // Could not resolve this subfolder (no IPNS record) — skip it.\n        if (!childState) continue;\n        if (entry.ipnsName === targetIpnsName) return childState;\n        stack.push(childState);\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Target not found anywhere under root.\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Create a new subfolder inside an existing folder."
}
```

420	   * @returns The loaded FolderState, or null if it cannot be bootstrapped
421	   * @internal
422	   */
423	  async ensureFolderLoaded(targetIpnsName: string): Promise<FolderState | null> {
424	    const existing = this.folderTree.get(targetIpnsName);
425	    if (existing) return existing;
426	
427	    // Cannot self-bootstrap without the root IPNS signing key.
428	    if (!this.internalRootIpnsKeypair) return null;
429	
430	    // 1. Ensure root is loaded. Root is special: it has no parent to unwrap its
431	    //    keys from, so they come from config (rootFolderKey + rootIpnsKeypair).
432	    const rootIpnsName = this.config.rootIpnsName;
433	    let root = this.folderTree.get(rootIpnsName);
434	    if (!root) {
435	      root = await this.loadFolder(
436	        rootIpnsName,
437	        this.internalRootFolderKey,
438	        this.internalRootIpnsKeypair
439	      );
440	      if (!root) return null;
441	    }
442	    if (rootIpnsName === targetIpnsName) return root;
443	
444	    // 2. DFS from root, unwrapping child keys and loading metadata until the
445	    //    target is found. `visited` guards against repeats and pathological
446	    //    cycles in folder metadata.
447	    const visited = new Set<string>([rootIpnsName]);
448	    const stack: FolderState[] = [root];
449	    while (stack.length > 0) {
450	      const current = stack.pop() as FolderState;
451	      for (const child of current.children) {
452	        if (child.type !== 'folder') continue;
453	        const entry = child as FolderEntry;
454	        if (visited.has(entry.ipnsName)) continue;
455	        visited.add(entry.ipnsName);
456	
457	        let childState = this.folderTree.get(entry.ipnsName);
458	        if (!childState) {
459	          // Unwrap this subfolder's keys with the vault keypair (ECIES), then
460	          // load its metadata. loadFolder adopts independent clones into the
461	          // folderTree (zeroed on destroy()); the transient unwrapped buffers
462	          // are left to GC, matching the existing loadFolder/registerFolder
463	          // paths which likewise don't eagerly zero their unwrapped inputs.
464	          const folderKey = await unwrapKey(
465	            hexToBytes(entry.folderKeyEncrypted),
466	            this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey
467	          );
468	          const ipnsPrivateKey = await unwrapKey(
469	            hexToBytes(entry.ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted),
470	            this.internalVaultKeypair.privateKey
471	          );
472	          childState = await this.loadFolder(entry.ipnsName, folderKey, {
473	            // Public key is derived from the private key at signing time.
474	            publicKey: new Uint8Array(0),
475	            privateKey: ipnsPrivateKey,
476	          });
477	        }
478	        // Could not resolve this subfolder (no IPNS record) — skip it.
479	        if (!childState) continue;
480	        if (entry.ipnsName === targetIpnsName) return childState;
481	        stack.push(childState);
482	      }
483	    }
484	
485	    // Target not found anywhere under root.
486	    return null;
487	  }
488	
489	  /**
490	   * Create a new subfolder inside an existing folder.
491	   *
492	   * Generates IPNS keypair and folder key, wraps with user's public key,
493	   * adds folder entry to parent metadata, publishes parent IPNS record,
494	   * and publishes empty folder metadata for the new subfolder.
```

  "file_path": "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/packages/sdk/src/__tests__/ensure-folder-loaded.test.ts",
  "content": "import { describe, it, expect, vi, beforeEach } from 'vitest';\nimport { CipherBoxClient } from '../client';\nimport { createTestConfig } from './helpers';\nimport type { FolderEntry, FolderMetadata } from '@cipherbox/core';\n\n// Mock sdk-core: keep everything real except loadFolderMetadata, which we drive\n// per-folder so the DFS walk has metadata to descend through.\nvi.mock('@cipherbox/sdk-core', async (importOriginal) => {\n  const actual = await importOriginal<typeof import('@cipherbox/sdk-core')>();\n  return {\n    ...actual,\n    loadFolderMetadata: vi.fn(),\n  };\n});\n\n// Mock crypto: ensureFolderLoaded unwraps each subfolder's keys with these.\n// Return value content is irrelevant — loadFolderMetadata is mocked, so the\n// \"decrypted\" key is never actually used to decrypt anything.\nvi.mock('@cipherbox/crypto', () => ({\n  unwrapKey: vi.fn().mockResolvedValue(new Uint8Array(32).fill(9)),\n  hexToBytes: vi.fn().mockReturnValue(new Uint8Array(32)),\n  clearBytes: vi.fn(),\n}));\n\nimport * as sdkCore from '@cipherbox/sdk-core';\n\nconst ROOT = 'k51test'; // matches createTestConfig().rootIpnsName\n\nfunction folderEntry(ipnsName: string, name: string): FolderEntry {\n  return {\n    type: 'folder',\n    id: `id-${ipnsName}`,\n    name,\n    ipnsName,\n    ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: 'aa',\n    folderKeyEncrypted: 'bb',\n    createdAt: 1,\n    modifiedAt: 1,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction metadata(children: FolderMetadata['children']): FolderMetadata {\n  return { version: 'v2', children };\n}\n\n/** Drive loadFolderMetadata to return canned metadata keyed by IPNS name. */\nfunction mockTree(tree: Record<string, FolderMetadata['children']>) {\n  vi.mocked(sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata).mockImplementation(async ({ ipnsName }) => {\n    const children = tree[ipnsName];\n    if (!children) return null;\n    return { metadata: metadata(children), sequenceNumber: 1n, cid: `cid-${ipnsName}` };\n  });\n}\n\nconst rootIpnsKeypair = {\n  publicKey: new Uint8Array(32).fill(7),\n  privateKey: new Uint8Array(64).fill(8),\n};\n\ndescribe('CipherBoxClient.ensureFolderLoaded', () => {\n  beforeEach(() => {\n    vi.clearAllMocks();\n  });\n\n  it('returns the existing state without resolving IPNS when already loaded', async () => {\n    const client = new CipherBoxClient(createTestConfig({ rootIpnsKeypair }));\n    client.getFolderTree().set('k51a', {\n      ipnsName: 'k51a',\n      folderKey: new Uint8Array(32).fill(1),\n      ipnsKeypair: { publicKey: new Uint8Array(0), privateKey: new Uint8Array(32).fill(2) },\n      sequenceNumber: 5n,\n      children: [],\n      metadata: null,\n      lastLoadedAt: 1,\n    });\n\n    const result = await client.ensureFolderLoaded('k51a');\n\n    expect(result?.ipnsName).toBe('k51a');\n    expect(result?.sequenceNumber).toBe(5n);\n    expect(sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it('returns null without resolving IPNS when no root IPNS keypair is configured', async () => {\n    const client = new CipherBoxClient(createTestConfig()); // no rootIpnsKeypair\n\n    const result = await client.ensureFolderLoaded('k51missing');\n\n    expect(result).toBeNull();\n    expect(sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata).not.toHaveBeenCalled();\n  });\n\n  it('bootstraps the root folder when the target IS the root', async () => {\n    const client = new CipherBoxClient(createTestConfig({ rootIpnsKeypair }));\n    mockTree({ [ROOT]: [] });\n\n    const result = await client.ensureFolderLoaded(ROOT);\n\n    expect(result?.ipnsName).toBe(ROOT);\n    expect(client.hasFolder(ROOT)).toBe(true);\n    expect(sdkCore.loadFolderMetadata).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);\n  });\n\n  it('walks from root down to a deep target, registering every folder on the path', async () => {\n    const client = new CipherBoxClient(createTestConfig({ rootIpnsKeypair }));\n    // root -> A -> B(target)\n    mockTree({\n      [ROOT]: [folderEntry('k51a', 'A')],\n      k51a: [folderEntry('k51b', 'B')],\n      k51b: [],\n    });\n\n    const result = await client.ensureFolderLoaded('k51b');\n\n    expect(result?.ipnsName).toBe('k51b');\n    // Whole path cached for cheap subsequent lookups.\n    expect(client.hasFolder(ROOT)).toBe(true);\n    expect(client.hasFolder('k51a')).toBe(true);\n    expect(client.hasFolder('k51b')).toBe(true);\n    // Keys for the two non-root subfolders were unwrapped with the vault keypair.\n    const crypto = await import('@cipherbox/crypto');\n    expect(vi.mocked(crypto.unwrapKey).mock.calls.length).toBe(4); // folderKey + ipnsKey per subfolder\n  });\n\n  it('returns null when the target is not reachable from root', async () => {\n    const client = new CipherBoxClient(createTestConfig({ rootIpnsKeypair }));\n    mockTree({\n      [ROOT]: [folderEntry('k51a', 'A')],\n      k51a: [], // no further descendants; target k51zzz absent\n    });\n\n    const result = await client.ensureFolderLoaded('k51zzz');\n\n    expect(result).toBeNull();\n    // Still cached what it walked.\n    expect(client.hasFolder('k51a')).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('short-circuits a subfolder that has no IPNS record (skips, keeps walking)', async () => {\n    const client = new CipherBoxClient(createTestConfig({ rootIpnsKeypair }));\n    // root has A (unresolvable) and B(target); A returns null from loadFolderMetadata.\n    mockTree({\n      [ROOT]: [folderEntry('k51a', 'A'), folderEntry('k51b', 'B')],\n      // k51a intentionally absent -> loadFolderMetadata returns null\n      k51b: [],\n    });\n\n    const result = await client.ensureFolderLoaded('k51b');\n\n    expect(result?.ipnsName).toBe('k51b');\n    expect(client.hasFolder('k51a')).toBe(false);\n    expect(client.hasFolder('k51b')).toBe(true);\n  });\n});\n"
}

**Navigation into folder (from `full-workflow.spec.ts` lines 185-195):**

```typescript
async function navigateIntoFolder(name: string): Promise<void> {
  // Retry once if navigation fails (transient IPNS failures in CI)
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 2; attempt++) {
    await fileList.doubleClickFolder(name);
    try {
      await breadcrumbs.waitForPathToContain(name, { timeout: 30000 });
      navigationStack.push(name);
      return;
    } catch {
      if (attempt === 1) throw new Error(`navigateIntoFolder("${name}") failed after 2 attempts`);
      await page.waitForTimeout(2000);
      await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(name, { timeout: 10000 });
    }
  }
}
```

**Usage snippet (from `full-workflow.spec.ts` lines 170-179):**

```typescript
async function createFolder(name: string): Promise<void> {
  const newFolderButton = page.locator('.file-browser-new-folder-button');
  await newFolderButton.click();
  await createFolderDialog.waitForOpen();
  await createFolderDialog.createFolder(name);
  await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(name, { timeout: 30000 });
  
  // Store folder ID immediately after creation
  folderIds[name] = await fileList.getItemId(name);
}
```

- Button to open dialog: `.file-browser-new-folder-button`
- Dialog input field: `#folder-name-input`
- Dialog: `.dialog-content` (filtered by has #folder-name-input)
- Create button: `.dialog-button--primary[type="submit"]`

### 6. Page Reload + Session Restore Recipe (from `full-workflow.spec.ts` lines 536-575)

**Exact reload pattern:**

```typescript
// Navigate to root before reload
await navigateToRoot();

// Reload page — Zustand store is wiped
await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });

// Reset navigation stack
navigationStack.length = 0;
navigationStack.push('root');

// Core Kit session auto-restores after reload.
// Wait for user menu (proves Core Kit re-init + token refresh complete)
await page.locator('[data-testid="user-menu"]').waitFor({
  state: 'visible',
  timeout: 120000,  // 2 minutes — Sapphire Devnet latency varies
});

// Wait for initial sync to complete — proves vault loaded + IPNS root re-fetched
await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(workspaceFolder, { timeout: 120000 });

// Wait for files too (sync may render folders before files)
await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(rootFiles[0].name, { timeout: 30000 });
```

**Key waiting elements:**
- `data-testid="user-menu"` — proves session restored (Core Kit init + token refresh)
- File list item appearance — proves vault + SDK initialized + IPNS metadata fetched

### 8. Test Data Structure & Imports

**Standard test setup (from `full-workflow.spec.ts` lines 1-58):**

```typescript
import { test, expect, Page, Browser, BrowserContext } from '@playwright/test';
import type { PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
import { createTestAccount, setupMockWallet, loginViaWallet } from '../utils/wallet-login-helpers';
import { createTestTextFile, cleanupTestFiles } from '../utils/test-files';
import { deleteAccountViaPage } from '../utils/cleanup-helpers';
import { FileListPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/file-list.page';
import { UploadZonePage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/upload-zone.page';
import { ContextMenuPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/context-menu.page';
import { BinPage } from '../page-objects/pages/bin.page';
import { CreateFolderDialogPage } from '../page-objects/dialogs/create-folder-dialog.page';

let browser: Browser;
let context: BrowserContext;
let page: Page;
let fileList: FileListPage;
let uploadZone: UploadZonePage;
let contextMenu: ContextMenuPage;
let binPage: BinPage;
let createFolderDialog: CreateFolderDialogPage;
let account: PrivateKeyAccount;

test.beforeAll(async ({ browser: testBrowser }) => {
  browser = testBrowser;
  context = await browser.newContext();
  page = await context.newPage();
  account = createTestAccount();
  await setupMockWallet(page, account);
  fileList = new FileListPage(page);
  uploadZone = new UploadZonePage(page);
  contextMenu = new ContextMenuPage(page);
  binPage = new BinPage(page);
  createFolderDialog = new CreateFolderDialogPage(page);
});

test.afterAll(async () => {
  cleanupTestFiles();
  if (page) await deleteAccountViaPage(page);
  if (context) await context.close();
});
```

### 9. Your Test Spec Pattern: Delete from Subfolder Without Navigating In

Based on the APIs above, here's the exact pattern for your scenario:

```typescript
import { test, expect, Page, Browser, BrowserContext } from '@playwright/test';
import type { PrivateKeyAccount } from 'viem/accounts';
import { createTestAccount, setupMockWallet, loginViaWallet } from '../utils/wallet-login-helpers';
import { createTestTextFile, cleanupTestFiles } from '../utils/test-files';
import { deleteAccountViaPage } from '../utils/cleanup-helpers';
import { FileListPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/file-list.page';
import { UploadZonePage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/upload-zone.page';
import { ContextMenuPage } from '../page-objects/file-browser/context-menu.page';
import { ConfirmDialogPage } from '../page-objects/dialogs/confirm-dialog.page';
import { BinPage } from '../page-objects/pages/bin.page';
import { CreateFolderDialogPage } from '../page-objects/dialogs/create-folder-dialog.page';

test.describe.serial('Delete from subfolder, restore without navigating in', () => {
  let page: Page;
  let fileList: FileListPage;
  let uploadZone: UploadZonePage;
  let contextMenu: ContextMenuPage;
  let confirmDialog: ConfirmDialogPage;
  let binPage: BinPage;
  let createFolderDialog: CreateFolderDialogPage;

  test.beforeAll(async ({ browser }) => {
    const context = await browser.newContext();
    page = await context.newPage();
    const account = createTestAccount();
    await setupMockWallet(page, account);
    
    fileList = new FileListPage(page);
    uploadZone = new UploadZonePage(page);
    contextMenu = new ContextMenuPage(page);
    confirmDialog = new ConfirmDialogPage(page);
    binPage = new BinPage(page);
    createFolderDialog = new CreateFolderDialogPage(page);

    await loginViaWallet(page, { timeout: 60_000 });
  });

  test('create subfolder, upload file in it, delete it, reload, and restore from bin', async () => {
    test.slow();

    const timestamp = Date.now();
    const subfolderName = `sub-${timestamp}`;
    const fileName = `file-${timestamp}.txt`;

    // 1. Create subfolder
    const newFolderButton = page.locator('.file-browser-new-folder-button');
    await newFolderButton.click();
    await createFolderDialog.waitForOpen();
    await createFolderDialog.createFolder(subfolderName);
    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(subfolderName, { timeout: 30000 });

    // 2. Navigate into subfolder
    await fileList.doubleClickFolder(subfolderName);
    await page.waitForTimeout(500); // Wait for navigation

    // 3. Upload file in subfolder
    const testFile = createTestTextFile(fileName, `Test file in ${subfolderName}`);
    await uploadZone.uploadFile(testFile.path);
    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });

    // 4. Delete file (sends to bin)
    await fileList.rightClickItem(fileName);
    await contextMenu.waitForOpen();
    await contextMenu.clickDelete();
    await confirmDialog.waitForOpen();
    await confirmDialog.clickConfirm();
    await fileList.waitForItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });

    // 5. Reload page
    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });
    await page.locator('[data-testid="user-menu"]').waitFor({
      state: 'visible',
      timeout: 120000,
    });

    // 6. Navigate to bin (WITHOUT navigating into subfolder)
    await binPage.navigate();
    await binPage.waitForBinItem(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });

    // 7. Restore file from bin
    await binPage.restoreItem(fileName);
    await binPage.waitForBinItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });

    // 8. Navigate back to files and verify restore
    await page.getByTestId('nav-item-files').click();
    await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 15000 });
    
    // Navigate into subfolder to verify file is restored there
    await fileList.doubleClickFolder(subfolderName);
    await page.waitForTimeout(500);
    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });
    expect(await fileList.isItemVisible(fileName)).toBe(true);

    cleanupTestFiles();
  });
});
```

After a reload only the root folder is re-seeded into the\n * SDK folderTree, so the subfolder's keys are absent — pre-fix, restoreFromBin\n * threw 'Target folder not loaded'.\n *\n * The fix gives the SDK client the root IPNS keypair so it can self-bootstrap:\n * client.restoreFromBin → ensureFolderLoaded walks from root, unwraps the\n * subfolder's keys, and loads it before republishing the parent.\n *\n * The existing recycle-bin.spec.ts misses this because it uploads in-session\n * (parent already loaded) and never reloads. This spec lives on its own with\n * its own login so nothing pre-seeds the subfolder into folderTree.\n */\ntest.describe.serial('Recycle Bin: restore after reload (subfolder parent)', () => {\n  let browser: Browser;\n  let context: BrowserContext;\n  let page: Page;\n\n  let fileList: FileListPage;\n  let uploadZone: UploadZonePage;\n  let contextMenu: ContextMenuPage;\n  let confirmDialog: ConfirmDialogPage;\n  let binPage: BinPage;\n  let createFolderDialog: CreateFolderDialogPage;\n\n  let account: PrivateKeyAccount;\n\n  test.beforeAll(async ({ browser: testBrowser }) => {\n    test.setTimeout(90_000); // Core Kit init + SIWE can be slow\n    browser = testBrowser;\n    context = await browser.newContext();\n    page = await context.newPage();\n\n    account = createTestAccount();\n    await setupMockWallet(page, account);\n\n    fileList = new FileListPage(page);\n    uploadZone = new UploadZonePage(page);\n    contextMenu = new ContextMenuPage(page);\n    confirmDialog = new ConfirmDialogPage(page);\n    binPage = new BinPage(page);\n    createFolderDialog = new CreateFolderDialogPage(page);\n\n    await loginViaWallet(page, { timeout: 60_000 });\n    await page.waitForURL('**/files', { timeout: 60000 });\n    await Promise.race([\n      fileList.fileListContainer().waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30000 }),\n      page.locator('[data-testid=\"empty-state\"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30000 }),\n    ]);\n  });\n\n  test.afterAll(async () => {\n    cleanupTestFiles();\n    if (page) {\n      await deleteAccountViaPage(page);\n    }\n    if (context) {\n      await context.close();\n    }\n  });\n\n  test('restores a subfolder item after reload without navigating into the subfolder', async () => {\n    test.slow(); // folder create + upload + delete + reload + restore IPNS cycles\n\n    const runId = Date.now().toString();\n    const subfolderName = `reload-sub-${runId}`;\n    const fileName = `reload-restore-${runId}.txt`;\n\n    // 1. Create a subfolder under root.\n    await page.locator('.file-browser-new-folder-button').click();\n    await createFolderDialog.waitForOpen();\n    await createFolderDialog.createFolder(subfolderName);\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(subfolderName, { timeout: 30000 });\n\n    // 2. Navigate into it (empty-state confirms we are inside the new folder).\n    await fileList.doubleClickFolder(subfolderName);\n    await page.locator('[data-testid=\"empty-state\"]').waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 30000 });\n\n    // 3. Upload a file into the subfolder.\n    const testFile = createTestTextFile(fileName, `restore-after-reload ${runId}`);\n    await uploadZone.uploadFile(testFile.path);\n    await fileList.waitForItemToAppear(fileName, { timeout: 60000 });\n\n    // 4. Delete it → moves to the recycle bin (originalParent = subfolder).\n    await fileList.rightClickItem(fileName);\n    await contextMenu.waitForOpen();\n    await contextMenu.clickDelete();\n    await confirmDialog.waitForOpen();\n    await confirmDialog.clickConfirm();\n    await fileList.waitForItemToDisappear(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });\n\n    // 5. Reload — wipes Zustand store AND the SDK folderTree. Only root is\n    //    re-seeded on session restore; the subfolder is NOT loaded.\n    await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });\n    await page\n      .locator('[data-testid=\"user-menu\"]')\n      .waitFor({ state: 'visible', timeout: 120000 }); // session auto-restore\n\n    // 6. Go straight to the bin WITHOUT navigating into the subfolder, so the\n    //    subfolder's keys are absent from folderTree at restore time.\n    await binPage.navigate();\n    await binPage.waitForBinItem(fileName, { timeout: 30000 });\n\n    // 7.
